to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{2}-P_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\text{\,.}

Overall we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathcal{T}}_{Q}(P_{1})-{\mathcal{T}}_{Q}(P_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{1}-P_{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\text{\,.}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{B}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{R^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}}{1-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{1}-P_{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{B}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{4}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{R^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}}{(1-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2})^{2}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{2}-P_{1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\left(1+\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{B}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{R^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}}{1-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}}\right)^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{1}-P_{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle=\gamma\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{1}-P_{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

as required.
The fact that the contraction constant
\gamma
is less than
1
follows from
Lemma

4
.
∎

Lemma

5
implies that the Riccati operator admits a unique fixed point that is within
{\mathcal{P}}
. This is summarized in the following corollary.

Corollary 2
.

For any
Q\in\Theta
the Riccati operator
{\mathcal{T}}_{Q}(\cdot)
(
Definition

2
) admits a unique fixed point
P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q)\in{\mathcal{P}}
.

Proof.

The space
\left(\mathbb{R}^{m,m},\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\cdot}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\right)
is complete, and
{\mathcal{P}}
is closed in this space because the PSD cone is closed and the condition
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{1-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}}
defines a closed set. Hence
\left({\mathcal{P}},\text{Dist}_{{\mathcal{P}}}(\cdot,\cdot)\right)
is a complete metric space. Therefore, since
{\mathcal{T}}_{Q}(\cdot)
is a contraction on
\left({\mathcal{P}},\text{Dist}_{{\mathcal{P}}}(\cdot,\cdot)\right)
(
Definition

4
) with contraction constant
\gamma\in[0,1)
given by
Lemma

5
, the Banach fixed point Theorem
[
10
]
guarantees that it admits a unique fixed point
P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q)\in{\mathcal{P}}
.
∎

We conclude this part of the proof by bounding the Lipschitz constant of the mapping to unique fixed points of the Riccati operator
{\mathcal{T}}_{Q}(\cdot)
.

Lemma 6
.

For any distinct
Q_{1},Q_{2}\in\Theta
, the mapping to unique fixed points of the Riccati operators
{\mathcal{T}}_{Q}(\cdot)
defined by
P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(\cdot)
(
Corollary

2
) satisfies

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Q_{1}-Q_{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\text{\,,}

where
\gamma
is the contraction constant of the Riccati operator given by
Lemma

5
.

Proof.

For any
Q\in\Theta
, the matrix
P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q)
is a fixed point of the Riccati operator
{\mathcal{T}}_{Q}(\cdot)
(
Definition

2
). Therefore by definition it holds that

\displaystyle P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})={\mathcal{T}}_{Q_{1}}(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))-{\mathcal{T}}_{Q_{2}}(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))\text{\,.}

Since
P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}),P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})\in{\mathcal{P}}
, we have that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{T}}_{Q_{1}}(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))-{\mathcal{T}}_{Q_{2}}(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))

\displaystyle=A^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})A+Q_{1}-A^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})BX(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})A

\displaystyle-A^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})A-Q_{2}+A^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})BX(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})A\text{\,.}

By bounding each of the three terms involving the fixed points as done in
Lemma

5
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{{\mathcal{T}}_{Q_{1}}(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))-{\mathcal{T}}_{Q_{2}}(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\leq\gamma\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Q_{1}-Q_{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\text{\,.}

Therefore, rearranging yields

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Q_{1}-Q_{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

as required.
∎

B.3
Lipschitz Bound on the Mapping

We conclude the proof of
Lemma

1
by converting the Lipschitz bound on the mapping to unique fixed points of the Riccati operator obtained in
Lemma

6
to a Lipschitz bound on the mapping
Q\mapsto K_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q)
.

Lemma

7
first provides a bound sharper than the one stated in
Lemma

1
, which we later simplify to the stated expression.

Lemma 7
.

For any distinct
Q_{1},Q_{2}\in\Theta
the mapping
Q\mapsto K_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q)
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{K_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-K_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{B}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{R^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\left(1+\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{B}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{R^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}}{1-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}}\right)\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Q_{1}-Q_{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

where
\gamma
is the contraction constant of the Riccati operator given in
Lemma

5
.

Proof.

By
Equation

11
and
Lemma

14
it holds that

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{K_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-K_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{-X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})A+X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

where
X(\cdot)
is as defined in
Equation

.
By the triangle inequality we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})-X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{B}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{R^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})-P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{B}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}}{1-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{A}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}-X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\text{\,,}

where we utilized the fact that
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2},\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{R^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}
established in
Lemma

5
and the fact that
P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}),P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})\in{\mathcal{P}}
.
Lastly, by
Lemmas

14
and
15
we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}-X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}(X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))-X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})))X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}(R+B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})B-R-B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})B)X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1}))^{-1}(B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})B-B^{\top}P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})B)X(P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2}))^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{B}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{R^{-1}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{2})-P_{\mathrm{unsafe}}(Q_{1})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{F}\text{\,.}

by simplifying the sharper upper bound for the Lipschitz constant provided in

to the stated expression.
